function getModelLibrary()
    return modelLibrary
end
function getCrewCardLibrary()
    return crewCardLibrary
end
function getUpgradeLibeary()
    log("hi")
    return upgradesLibeary
end

function retriveModel(ref)
    if modelLibrary[ref]~= nil then
        return modelLibrary[ref]
    else
        return "na"
    end
end
function retriveCrewCard(ref)
    if crewCardLibrary[ref]~= nil then
        return crewCardLibrary[ref]
    else
        return "na"
    end
end
function hello_world()
    return "Hello World"
end
